{D3802}]@/_/stong phrag brgya pa'i rnam par bshad pa bzhugs/_@##/_/stong phrag brgya pa'i rnam par bshad pa zhes bya ba/_'phags pa 'jam dpal 
[\[204a.4\]]la phyag 'tshal lo/_/nyan thos zhi ba tshol rnams kun shes nyid kyis nyer zhir khrid mdzad gang yin dang /_/'gro la phan par bzhed rnams lam shes nyid kyis 'jig rten don sgrub mdzad pa gang /_/gang dag yang dag ldan rnams thub rnams sna tshogs 'di dag rnam pa kun gsungs pa/_/
[\[204a.5\]]sangs rgyas nyan thos byang chub sems dpa'i tshogs dang ldan yum de la phyag 'tshal lo/_/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'i ston lugs ni gnyis te/_sngon byung yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas sprul pa pos ji ltar mdzad pa dang /_rjes su 'jug pa mngon par rtogs pa'i rim pa'o/_/
[\[204a.6\]]sngon byung la gnyis/_mdo yon tan rin po che 'byung ba'i rgyu gleng gzhi phun sum tshogs pa dang /_mdo yon tan rin po che 'byung ba'i rkyen gleng bslang ba'o/_/gleng gzhi la gnyis las/_thun mong gi gleng gzhi ni 'di skad bdag gis thos pa'i dus gcig na/_bcom ldan 'das rgyal po'i 
[\[204a.7\]]khab bya rgod phung po'i ri la dge slong lnga stong tsam gyi dge slong gi dge 'dun chen po dang /_de la sogs pa phun sum tshogs pa lngas bstan to/_/thun mong ma yin pa'i gleng gzhi ni 'khor thun mong ma yin pa dang /_ston pa thun mong ma yin pas bstan to/_/'khor la gnyis las/_nyan thos 
[\[204b\]]
[\[204b.1\]]zhi ba tshol ba ni dge slong gi dge 'dun no/_/nye bar zhi bar khrid pa ni dgra bcom pa la sogs pa'i yon tan bcu bzhi'o/_/kun shes pa'i shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa ni lam mo/_/'gro la phan par byed pa ni byang chub sems dpa' bzang skyong la sogs pa'o/_/'jig rten don sgrub mdzad pa ni 
[\[204b.2\]]thams cad kyang gzungs rab tu thob pa la sogs pa'i yon tan no/_/lam shes pa nyid ni lam mo/_/ston pa thun mong ma yin pa ni bcom ldan 'das nyid kyis gdan seng ge'i khri bshams/_rdo rje'i skyil krung bcas/_sku drang por bsrang /_dgongs pa mngon du bzhag pa ni thub rnams 
[\[204b.3\]]zhes pa'o/_/rnam pa kun ldan sna tshogs 'di gsungs zhes pas ni cho 'phrul rnam pa gsum bstan pa las/_ting nge 'dzin gyi cho 'phrul gyis snod kyi 'jig rten byi dor dang /_bcud kyi sems can yongs su smin par mdzad pa gnyis las/_ting nge 'dzin gyi rgyal po dang /_seng ge rnam 
[\[204b.4\]]par rol pa'o/_/ting nge 'dzin gyi rgyal po la bzhi/_sku thams cad nas 'od zer rab tu bkye ba dang /_ba spu'i bu ga dang /_rang bzhin gyi 'od zer dang /_ljags kyi dbang po las 'od zer bkye ba'o/_/seng ge rnam par rol pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyis sems can yongs su sbyong ba la lnga/_ngan 'gro'i 
[\[204b.5\]]rgyud yongs su sbyang ba dang /_mi'i skyon sbyang ba dang /_phan sems bskyed pa dang /_dge sems bskyed pa dang /_de la ched du brjod pa'o/_/rdzu 'phrul gyi cho 'phrul la bzhi/_rdzu 'phrul gyis zil gyis mnan pa dang /_gdul bya mos par bya ba dang /_mchod pa byin gyis brlab pa dang /_gdul bya skal 
[\[204b.6\]]ba dang ldan par bstan pa'o/_/'khor sdud pa'i cho 'phrul la/_zhing khams bcu/_de bzhin gshegs pa bcu/_byang chub sems dpa' bcu/_dris pa bcu/_lan bcu/_zhus pa bcu/_gnang ba bcu/_'di nyid du byon nas bsnyen bkur byas pa bcu ni shar phyogs kyi 'jig rten gyi khams rin chen 
[\[204b.7\]]yod pa zhes bya ba na/_de bzhin gshegs pa dkon mchog 'byung gnas zhes bya ba bzhugs shing 'tsho gzhes te/_de na byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po kun nas 'od zer zhes bya ba 'dug 'dug pas/_'od chen po 'jig rten du byung ba dang /_dog sa cher g.yos pa dang /_de bzhin 
[\[205a\]]
[\[205a.1\]]gshegs pa'i sku byad lta bas chog mi shes pa mthong nas/_'di ci'i rgyu ci'i rkyen dris pas/_'di ni nub phyogs logs na 'jig rten gyi khams gang gA'i klung gi bye ma snyed 'das pa kun gyi pha rol na/_'jig rten gyi khams mi mjed ces bya ba yod de/_de na de bzhin 
[\[205a.2\]]gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas shAkya thub pa zhes bya ba bzhugs shing 'tsho skyong ngo /_/de byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams la shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa ston te/_'di ni de'i mthu'o/_/byang chub sems dpa' kun nas 'od zer gyis bdag cag 
[\[205a.3\]]kyang de bzhin gshegs pa blta ba'i phyir mchi'o zhes zhus pas/_gnang ba ni khyod de'i dus la bab par shes na song shig la 'jig rten gyi khams der bag yod par spyod cig ces pa la sogs pa'o/_/de nas shar phyogs kyi sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das la mchod pa byas nas byang chub 
[\[205a.4\]]sems dpa'i rdzu 'phrul dang mthu chen pos/_bcom ldan 'das shAkya thub pa ga la ba der byon te/_bcom ldan 'das la bsnyun mi mnga' 'am zhes gsol nas me tog gtor ro/_/bcom ldan 'das kyis me tog bzhes nas shar phyogs kyi logs su gtor ro/_/shar phyogs kyi 'jig rten gyi 
[\[205a.5\]]khams de rnams me tog pad+mas khebs par gyur nas/_me tog de dag la de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku mang po bzhugs shing pha rol tu phyin pa drug gi chos ston to/_/chos de sems can gang dag gis thos pa de dag thams cad kyang bla na med pa'i byang chub thob par gyur to/_/byang chub sems 
[\[205a.6\]]dpa' khyim pa dang rab tu byung ba'am/_khye'u dang bu mo'i gzugs kyis rang rang gi dge ba'i rtsa bas mchod pa byas nas phyogs gcig tu 'khod par gyur to/_/de bzhin du phyogs gzhan la bsgre'o/_/de'i tshe de'i dus na gzhi rin po che'i rang bzhin du gyur/_me tog [(]sel [,]sil [)]ma sna tshogs dang /_
[\[205a.7\]]dar gyi lda ldi dang /_spos kyi shing dang dpag bsam gyi shing rtse mo g.yur za ba sna tshogs dper na byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po me tog kun nas rgyas pa'i zhing gang na/_'phags pa 'jam dpal gzhon nur gyur pa bzhugs pa'am/_lha'i bu shin tu brtan pa bzhugs pa'i 
[\[205b\]]
[\[205b.1\]]'jig rten gyi khams pad+ma can bzhin du gyur to/_/gang dag yang dag ldan pa ni rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid de/_yum de gsum la phyag 'tshal ba'o/_/de yang gleng gzhi rgyas par bka' stsal sdud par byed pa mchod brjod tshig le'ur bcas pa mdzad ces pa'o/_/gleng gzhi'i 
[\[205b.2\]]le'u ste dang po'o//_//mdo yon tan rin po che 'byung ba'i rkyen gleng bslang ba ni gnyis/_ston pas gleng bslang ba dang /_'khor gyis [(]glang [,]gleng [)]bslang ba'o/_/ston pas gleng bslang ba ni de'i tshe bcom ldan 'das kyis lha dang bcas pa/_bdud dang bcas pa/_dge sbyong dang bram 
[\[205b.3\]]ze dang byang chub sems dpa' gzhon nur gyur pa mang po stong gsum gyi stong 'dir 'dus par thugs su chud nas/_tshe dang ldan pa shA ri'i bu la bka' stsal pa/_shA ri'i bu byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po chos thams cad rnam pa thams cad du mngon par rdzogs par sangs 
[\[205b.4\]]rgyas par 'dod pas shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la bslab par bya'o zhes pa'o/_/'khor gyis gleng bslang ba ni mdo don gzhung gnas skabs su ston to/_/dgos 'brel ni/_rnam pa thams cad mkhyen nyid lam/_/ston pas 'di las bshad pa gang /_/gzhan gyis myong ba ma 
[\[205b.5\]]yin de/_/blo dang ldan pas mthong 'gyur phyir/_/chos spyod bcu yi bdag nyid kyi/_/mdo don dran pa la bzhag nas/_/bde blag tu ni rtogs 'gyur ba/_/bya ba rtsom pa'i dgos pa yin/_/zhes pa la/_rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid ni dgos pa'i dgos pa'o/_/brjod bya 
[\[205b.6\]]ni shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin ma'o/_/dgos pa ni brjod bya rtogs pa'o/_/'brel pa ni thabs dang thabs las byung ba'o/_/mngon rtogs mdzad pa'i dgos pa ni don de nyid 'phags pa thogs med la sogs pas bde blag tu rtogs pa'i thabs su mdzad pa'o/_/rjes su 'jug pa la gnyis 
[\[205b.7\]]lus rnam par gzhag pa dang /_yan lag rgyas par bshad pa'o/_/lus rnam par gzhag pa ni yum chen mo 'di la tshigs su bcad pa stong phrag brgya tham pa/_bam po sum brgya/_zhus lan brgya nyi shu/_rnam grangs bcu gcig_/'gres rkang brgya rtsa brgyad/_'gres rtsa sum brgya 
[\[206a\]]
[\[206a.1\]]drug cu/_bshad thabs rnam pa lnga/_bshad pa dkyus thog_/gang zag nyams thog_/chos nyid don thog dang gsum/_skabs brgyad kyis mdor bstan/_skabs bu chung bdun cus rgyas par bshad/_gzhung bye brag tu phye bas shin tu rgyas par bshad do/_/
[\[206a.2\]]nye bar bsdu ba la gnyis/_drug gis gnas skabs kyi bsdu ba/_gsum gyis rtsa ba'i bsdu ba'o/_/skabs brgyad ni/_shes rab pha rol phyin pa la/_/dngos po brgyad kyis yang dag bshad/_/rnam kun mkhyen nyid lam shes nyid/_/de nas thams cad shes pa nyid/_/rnam kun 
[\[206a.3\]]mngon rdzogs rtogs pa dang /_/rtse mor phyin dang mthar gyis pa/_/skad cig gcig mngon rdzogs byang chub/_/chos kyi sku dang de rnam brgyad/_/ces pa'o/_/de la lta sgom rnam pa bzhi/_ji lta ba dang ji snyed pa'i chos rang bzhin med par rtogs pa ni rnam pa skye med do/_/'phags pa'i 
[\[206a.4\]]gang zag thams cad kyi lam rnams rang bzhin med par rtogs pa ni lam skye med do/_/gzhi phung po khams la sogs pa gdod ma nas rang bzhin med par shes pa ni gzhi skye med do/_/de gsum gyi gzhi dngos por sgro 'dogs pa dang /_lam sems nyid du zhen pa dang /_'bras bu 
[\[206a.5\]]rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid la re ba'i sgro 'dogs gsum chod pas gtan la phebs pa'o/_/sgom pa la gnyis/_thun sgom du de gsum rdzogs par sgom pa ni rnam kun mngon rdzogs so/_/de nyid rtogs pa la skyed byung nas 'bad rtsol mi dgos par sgom pa ni rtse mo mngon 
[\[206a.6\]]rtogs so/_/spyod pa la gnyis/_pha rol tu phyin pa drug rim gyis 'char ba ni mthar gyis pa/_'bral med du bsgoms pas pha rol tu phyin pa drug cig car du rdzogs pa ni skad cig ma gcig la mngon par rdzogs par byang chub pa'o/_/chos sku mdzad pa dang bcas pa ni bdag don gzhan 
[\[206a.7\]]don phun sum tshogs pa ste/_'bras bu'o/_/skabs bu chung bdun cu ni/_sems bskyed pa dang gdams ngag dang /_/nges 'byed yan lag rnam bzhi dang /_/sgrub pa yi ni rten gyur pa/_/chos kyi dbyings kyi rang bzhin dang /_/dmigs pa dag dang ched dang ni/_/go cha 'jug pa'i bya ba dang /_/
[\[206b\]]
[\[206b.1\]]tshogs ni nges par 'byung bcas rnams/_/thub pa'i rnam kun mkhyen pa nyid/_/mog mog por byed la sogs dang /_/slob ma bse ru'i lam gang dang /_/'di dang gzhan pa'i phan yon gyis/_/phan yon che ba mthong ba'i lam/_/byed pa dang ni mos pa dang /_/bstod dang bkur dang 
[\[206b.2\]]bsngags pa dang /_/bsngo dang rjes su yi rang dang /_/yid la byed pa bla med dag_/sgrub dang shin tu dag pa zhes/_/bya ba 'di ni bsgom pa'i lam/_/byang chub sems dpa' mkhas rnams kyi/_/lam shes nyid ni de 'drar bshad/_/shes pas srid la mi gnas shing /_/snying rjes zhi la 
[\[206b.3\]]mi gnas dang /_/thabs ma yin pas ring ba dang /_/thabs kyis ring ba ma yin nyid/_/mi mthun gnyen po'i phyogs dag dang /_/sbyor dang de yi mnyam nyid dang /_/nyan thos la sogs mthong ba'i lam/_/de 'drar kun shes nyid du 'dod/_/rnam pa sbyor bar bcas rnams dang /_/
[\[206b.4\]]yon tan skyon ni mtshan nyid bcas/_/mthar dang nges 'byed cha mthun dang /_/slob pa phyir mi ldog pa'i tshogs/_/srid dang zhi ba mnyam nyid dang /_/zhing dag bla na med pa dang /_/'di ni thabs mkhas bcas pa yin/_/de yis rtags dang rnam 'phel dang /_/brtan dang sems kun 
[\[206b.5\]]gnas pa dang /_/mthong zhes bya dang bsgom pa zhes/_/bya ba'i lam ni so so la/_/rnam par rtog pa rnam bzhi yi/_/gnyen po rnam pa bzhi dang ni/_/bar chad med pa'i ting nge 'dzin/_/log par sgrub dang bcas pa ni/_/rtse mo'i mngon rtogs mthar gyis pa/_/rnam gsum 
[\[206b.6\]]rnam bcu skad cig ma/_/gcig gis mngon rdzogs byang chub pa/_/mtshan nyid kyis ni rnam pa bzhi/_/ngo bo nyid longs rdzogs bcas pa/_/de bzhin gzhan ni sprul sku dang /_/chos sku mdzad par bcas pa ste/_/rnam pa bzhir ni yang dag brjod/_/ces pa ni bsdus pa'i don 
[\[206b.7\]]no/_/dgos pa'i don ni bshad pa pos bshad sla ba/_nyan pa pos rtogs sla ba'o/_/tshig gi don ni gzhung dang sbyar ro/_/le'u 'di nas shA ri'i bu'i zhus lan gsum/_'khor spyi'i zhus lan dang bzhi/_zhal gyi sgo nas ljags kyi dbang po brkyang nas rab 'byor la chos ston par bskul 
[\[207a\]]
[\[207a.1\]]ba ni bsdus pa'i don to/_/zhus lan dang po ni shA ri'i bus sangs rgya bar 'dod pas zhus lan la sems bskyed/_zhus lan gnyis pa shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la ji ltar brtson gyi zhus lan la gdams ngag rang gi ngo bo/_dmigs pa bden pa bzhi/_
[\[207a.2\]]rten sangs rgyas dang chos la gdams pa'o/_/zhus lan gsum pa shA ri'i bus byang chub sems dpa' shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la spyod pa de dag gang nas tshe 'phos nas 'dir 'ongs/_'di nas gar skye zhes zhus pa'i lan du dge 'dun nyi shu/_ma zhen/_mi ngal/_lam 
[\[207a.3\]]yongs su 'dzin pa/_spyan lnga dang /_mngon par shes pa drug la gdams pa'o/_/zhus lan bzhi pa/_'khor spyis shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la spyod pas pha rol tu phyin pa drug rdzogs nas/_mngon par rdzogs par sangs rgyas par 'gyur ro zhus pas/_de'i lan 'thad pa dang 
[\[207a.4\]]bcas pa gsungs so/_/sems bskyed pa ni gzhan don phyir/_/yang dag rdzogs pa'i byang chub 'dod/_/ces pa ni sems can gyi don du rdzogs pa'i byang chub 'dod pa ste/_sems bskyed kyi ngo bo'o/_/de dang de ni mdo bzhin du/_/bsdus dang rgyas pa'i sgo nas brjod/_/ces pa la/_
[\[207a.5\]]de dang gzhan don de ni byang chub mdo bzhin ni bsdus pa nas smon 'jug gnyis su gsungs/_mdo rgyas pa nas dgos pa'i sgo nas nyi shu rtsa gnyis gsungs pa ni/_de yang sa gser zla ba me/_/gter dang rin chen 'byung gnas mtsho/_/rdo rje ri sman bshes gnyen dang /_/yid bzhin nor bu nyi ma glu/_/
[\[207a.6\]]rgyal po mdzod dang lam po che/_/bzhon pa bkod ma'i chu dang ni/_/sgra snyan chu bo sprin rnams kyis/_/rnam pa nyi shu rtsa gnyis so/_/zhes pa las/_'dun pa sa lta bu'o/_/byang chub kyi sems ni byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po chos thams cad rnam pa thams cad du 
[\[207a.7\]]mngon par rdzogs par 'tshang rgya bar 'dod pas shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la brtson par bya'o/_/dge ba'i lo tog thams cad smin pas sa dang 'dra'o/_/bsam pa bzang po gser lta bu'i byang chub kyi sems ni sbyin pa dang gtong ba dang len pa mi dmigs pa'i phyir sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa 
[\[207b\]]
[\[207b.1\]]yongs su rdzogs par 'dod pa la sogs pas yum la bslab par bya'o/_/nam yang pha rol tu phyin pa drug gi bsam pa mi 'gyur bas gser dang 'dra'o/_/lhag pa'i bsam pa zla ba tshes pa lta bu'i byang chub kyi sems ni dran pa nye bar gzhag pa nas sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa bco brgyad 
[\[207b.2\]]kyi bar du mi dmigs pa'i tshul gyis yongs su rdzogs par 'dod pas yum la bslab par bya'o/_/dge ba'i rtsa ba rdzogs par gong nas gong du 'phel bas zla ba tshes pa lta bu'o/_/sbyor ba me lta bu'i byang chub kyi sems ni rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid thob par 'dod pa dang /_lam rdzogs 
[\[207b.3\]]par 'dod pa dang /_gzhi thams cad shes par 'dod pa dang /_bag chags kyi mtshams sbyor ba yongs su spang bar 'dod pas yum la bslab par bya'o/_/mkhyen pa gsum gyi mi mthun pa'i phyogs kyi bud shing bsregs pas me lta bu'o/_/sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa gter lta bu'i byang chub 
[\[207b.4\]]kyi sems ni ser sna can rnams sbyin pa la 'god par 'dod pa dang /_nyan thos dang rang sangs rgyas kyi pha rol tu phyin pa drug zil gyis mnan par 'dod pas yum la bslab par bya'o/_/sbyin pa mi zad pa gter chen po lta bu'o/_/tshul khrims kyi pha rol tu phyin pa rin po che'i 'byung khungs bzang po 
[\[207b.5\]]lta bu'i byang chub kyi sems ni tshul khrims 'chal pa rnams tshul khrims la 'god par 'dod pas yum la bslab par bya'o/_/mngon mtho dang nges legs tshul khrims las 'byung bas rin po che'i 'byung khungs bzang po lta bu'o/_/bzod pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa rgya mtsho lta bu'i byang chub kyi sems ni 
[\[207b.6\]]sems 'khrugs pa rnams bzod pa la 'god par 'dod pas yum la bslab par bya'o/_/bzod pa de zab pas rgya mtsho lta bu'o/_/brtson 'grus kyi pha rol tu phyin pa rdo rje lta bu'i byang chub kyi sems ni le lo can rnams brtson 'grus la dgod par 'dod pas yum la bslab par bya'o/_/brtson 
[\[207b.7\]]'grus kyi pha rol tu phyin pa de la mi dge bas dbye bar mi nus pas rdo rje pha lam lta bu'o/_/bsam gtan gyi pha rol tu phyin pa ri'i rgyal po lta bu'i byang chub kyi sems ni sems g.yeng ba rnams bsam gtan la dgod par 'dod pas yum la bslab par bya'o/_/bsam gtan gyi pha rol tu phyin pa 
[\[208a\]]
[\[208a.1\]]mtshan ma dang mtshan ma med pas mi sgul bas ri rab lta bu'o/_/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa sman bzang po lta bu'i byang chub kyi sems ni shes rab 'chal pa rnams shes rab la 'god par 'dod pa dang /_nyan thos dang rang rgyal gyi shes rab zil gyis mnan 
[\[208a.2\]]par 'dod pas yum la bslab par bya'o/_/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa nyon mongs pa'i nad thams cad zhi bar byed pas sman lta bu'o/_/thabs kyi pha rol tu phyin pa dge ba'i bshes gnyen lta bu'i byang chub kyi sems ni pha rol tu phyin pa drug thabs mkhas pas chung ngu yang rnam pa 
[\[208a.3\]]thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi bar du mi zad par 'dod pas yum la bslab bar bya'o/_/thabs kyi pha rol tu phyin pa gang yang dge ba'i grogs su 'char bas dge ba'i bshes gnyen lta bu'o/_/smon lam gyi pha rol tu phyin pa yid bzhin gyi nor bu rin po che lta bu'i byang chub kyi sems ni 
[\[208a.4\]]tshe rabs thams cad du bdag gis sangs rgyas kyi sku mthong ba dang /_sangs rgyas kyi sa dgongs shing myur du de bzhin gshegs pa'i sa rtogs par 'dod pas yum la bslab par bya'o/_/smon lam gyi pha rol tu phyin pa dgos 'dod thams cad 'byung bas rin po che lta bu'o/_/
[\[208a.5\]]stobs kyi pha rol tu phyin pa nyi ma'i snying po lta bu'i byang chub kyi sems ni bdag gis sangs rgyas thams cad kyi chos thams cad rdzogs par 'dod pa dang /_sems can thams cad dge ba la gzud par 'dod pas yum la bslab par bya'o/_/stobs kyi pha rol tu phyin pa gdul bya yongs 
[\[208a.6\]]su smin par byed pas nyi ma lta bu'o/_/ye shes kyi pha rol tu phyin pa glu dbyangs snyan pa lta bu'i byang chub kyi sems ni phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten gyi khams su sems skyed pa gcig gis 'dong zhing yon tan brjod pa dang /_sgra dbyangs gcig gis go bar 'dod pa dang /_stong pa bco brgyad 
[\[208a.7\]]shes par 'dod pa dang /_de bzhin nyid la sogs pa'i chos thams cad kyi mtshan nyid khong du chud par 'dod pas yum la bslab par bya'o/_/ye shes kyi pha rol tu phyin pa gdul bya rnams kyi dang ba 'dren par byed pa'i dri za'i glu dbyangs snyan pa lta bu'o/_/mngon par shes pa rgyal po lta bu'i byang 
[\[208b\]]
[\[208b.1\]]chub kyi sems ni stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams shes par 'dod/_chu thams cad skra'i nyag ma gcig gis blang bar 'dod/_me'i phung po thams cad zhi bar 'dod/_rlung gi dkyil 'khor thams cad zhi bar 'dod/_skyil mo krung gcig gis khengs par 'dod/_skra nyag ma

[\[217b\]]
[\[217b.1\]]thabs mkhas par shes par bya/_rab 'byor gyis smras pa/_byang chub sems dpa' yum la spyod pa'i tshe/_gzugs rtag pa dang mi rtag pa dang /_bde sdug dang /_bdag yod med dang /_zhi ma zhi dang /_stong mi stong dang /_mtshan ma yod med dang /_smon pa yod med dang /_dben pa dang mi dben pa dang /_de 
[\[217b.2\]]bzhin du chos thams cad la mi spyod pa'i tshe/_thabs mkhas par shes par bya'o/_/de cis na/_gzugs kyi stong pa nyid gang yin pa de gzugs ma yin zhes pa las snang stong bzhi sbyor dang /_spyod mi spyod la sogs pa mu bzhi ru mi dmigs te chos thams cad dngos po med par khong du chud pa'i phyir ro/_/chos 
[\[217b.3\]]mchog gi ting nge 'dzin ni/_ting nge 'dzin de'i byed pa nyid/_/lung ston pa dang rlom pa zad/_/gsum po phan tshun ngo bo gcig_/ting 'dzin rnam par mi rtog pa/_/de ltar nges 'byed cha mthun ni/_/chung dang 'bring dang chen po yin/_/zhes pas chos mchog chung ngu'i dmigs rnams ting nge 'dzin de'i byed pa 
[\[217b.4\]]ni chos thams cad skye ba med pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin yod de/_ting nge 'dzin de rnam par spyod pa ni myur du bla na med par sangs rgya'o/_/skye ba med pa'i ting nge 'dzin de nyid la dpa' bar 'gro ba la sogs pa'i ting nge 'dzin brgya nyi shu kun la sbyar ro/_/chos mchog 'bring gi dmigs rnams lung ston pa 
[\[217b.5\]]dang rlom pa zad pa ni de nas sangs rgyas kyi mthus rab 'byor gyis smras pa/_byang chub sems dpa' ting nge 'dzin de dag gi spyod pa ni 'das pa dang da ltar gyi sangs rgyas rnams kyis lung bstan te/_ting nge 'dzin dang bdag dang snyoms par 'jug pa la rlom sems med do/_/rab 'byor gyis smras pa/_ting nge 
[\[217b.6\]]'dzin dang shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin ma dang byang chub sems dpa' ngo bo gcig pa ni chos mchog chen po'i dmigs pa'o/_/de rnams la rnam par mi rtog pa ni rnam pa'o/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis legs zhes gnang ba ni/_bka' stsal pa/_ngas nyon mongs med par bshad pa ni 'di yin no/_/
[\[217b.7\]]byang chub sems dpas kyang pha rol tu phyin pa nas sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa'i bar la de ltar bslab par bya'o/_/de nas shA ri'i bus gsol pa/_pha rol tu phyin pa nas sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa'i bar la mi dmigs pa'i tshul gyis slob pa lags sam/_bka' stsal pa/_de ltar slob pa ni mi dmigs 
[\[218a\]]
[\[218a.1\]]pa'i tshul gyis slob pa yin no/_/ci'i phyir na mi dmigs pa lags zhes pa'i lan byed pa'i skyes bu bcu gnyis nas shin tu rnam par dag pa'i phyir gzugs la sogs pa mi dmigs so/_/rnam par dag pa gang lags zhes pa'i lan chos thams cad skye 'gag med/_kun nas 
[\[218a.2\]]nyon mongs dang rnam byang med pa ni rnam par dag pa ste/_de ltar slob pa'i byang chub sems dpa' ni gang la yang mi slob bo/_/gzung ba'i rnam par rtog pa las/_/gzhi dang de yi gnyen po yis/_/gzung bar rtog pa rnam pa gnyis/_/rmongs dang phung sogs bye brag gis/_/de ni so sor rnam pa dgu/_/
[\[218a.3\]]zhes pa las/_rnam rtog gi go ba ni byis pa so so'i skye bo rnams 'khor 'das la zhen pa'i cha nas ni rtog pa la/_de gsal snang gi cha nas ni ye shes so/_/gzhi'i rtog pa dgu las/_dang po ma rig pa'i rtog pa ni shA ri'i bus chos 'di dag byis pa so so'i skye bos ji ltar mngon par chags pa de ltar yod pa 
[\[218a.4\]]ma yin no/_/gnyis pa gzugs la sogs pa'i rtog pa ni gzugs nas sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa bco brgyad kyi bar la/_stong pa bco brgyad kyi phyir yod pa ma yin no/_/gsum pa ming dang gzugs kyi rtog pa ni med pa la btags nas ming dang gzugs la mngon par zhen no/_/bzhi pa mtha' 
[\[218a.5\]]gnyis la chags pa'i rtog pa ni med pa la zhen cing rtag chad kyi mtha' mi shes so/_/lnga pa mi shes pa'i rtog pa ni gzugs nas sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa bco brgyad kyi bar mi shes mi mthong /_drug pa nges par mi 'byung ba'i rtog pa ni de khams gsum dang nyan rang las nges par mi 'byung ba'o/_/bdun 
[\[218a.6\]]pa yid mi ches pa'i rtog pa ni gzugs ni gzugs kyis stong zhes bya bar yid mi ches pa la sogs pa'o/_/brgyad pa mi gnas pa'i rtog pa ni sbyin pa nas rnam mkhyen gyi bar la gnas pa ma yin no/_/dgu pa mngon par zhen pa'i rtog pa ni gzugs nas rnam mkhyen gyi bar la mngon par zhen pas shes rab kyi pha 
[\[218a.7\]]rol tu phyin pa la mi spyod cing /_rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid du nges par mi 'byung ba'o/_/gnyen po'i rtog pa dgu las dang po ni shA ri'i bus gsol pa/_ji ltar bslabs na shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la bslabs shing rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid du nges par 'byung ba lags/_lam ni pha rol tu phyin 
[\[218b\]]
[\[218b.1\]]pa rnams ni mi dmigs dang po'o/_/gnyis pa stong pa bco brgyad mi dmigs/_gsum pa byang chub kyi phyogs kyi chos sum cu rtsa bdun mi dmigs/_bzhi pa 'phags pa'i bden pa la sogs pa mi dmigs/_lnga pa ting nge 'dzin dang gzungs kyi sgo dang mngon par shes pa rnams mi dmigs/_drug pa stobs 
[\[218b.2\]]la sogs pa rnams mi dmigs/_bdun pa thams cad shes pa mi dmigs/_brgyad pa lam gyi rnam pa shes pa nyid mi dmigs/_/dgu pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid mi dmigs shing yang dag par rjes su mi mthong na shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la slob cing rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid du nges 
[\[218b.3\]]par 'byung bar 'gyur ro/_/mtshan ma med pa'i le'u ste drug pa'o//_//le'u 'di nas 'dzin pa'i rnam par rtog pa gnyis/_thabs la mkhas pa dang ldan pa'i dge ba'i bshes gnyen/_thabs mi mkhas pa dang ldan pa'i sdig pa'i grogs po'o/_/de yang /_bdag sogs rang dbang ngo bo dang /_/
[\[218b.4\]]de bzhin phung sogs rten sgo nas/_/rdzas dang btags pa'i rten can gyi/_/'dzin pa rnam pa gnyis su 'dod/_/ces pa la/_gang zag gi rdzas kyi rtog pa ni/_shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la bslab rgyu med pa'o/_/skyes bu btags pa'i rtog pa ni nges par 'byung rgyu med par ston no/_/rtog pa dang po 
[\[218b.5\]]ni rab 'byor gyis gsol pa la la zhig 'di skad du 'dri zhing /_sgyu ma'i skyes bu 'di pha rol tu phyin pa drug nas sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa bco brgyad kyi bar la bslabs shing rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid du 'byung bar 'gyur ram/_de skad du 'dri ba de la ji skad du bstan par bgyi/_bka' stsal pa/_
[\[218b.6\]]khyod la dri yis khyed kyis ji skad du bstan pa de ltar lan thob cig_/rtog pa dang phung po'ang gzhan las sgyu ma'ang gzhan zhig yin snyam mam/_gnyis pa khams/_gsum pa skye mched/_bzhi pa rten 'brel/_lnga pa pha rol tu phyin pa rnams/_drug pa stong pa rnams/_bdun pa byang chub kyi phyogs kyis chos 
[\[218b.7\]]rnams/_brgyad pa 'phags pa'i bden pa la sogs pa/_dgu pa stobs rnams sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa bco brgyad do/_/rab 'byor gyis gsol pa/_gzugs kyang gzhan ma lags/_sgyu ma 'ang gzhan ma lags/_gzugs nyid kyang sgyu ma sgyu ma nyid kyang gzugs so/_/de bzhin du kun la 
[\[219a\]]
[\[219a.1\]]sbyar ro/_/skyes bu btags pa'i rtog pa ni nges par 'byung rgyu med par ston pa las dang po'i sgyu ma la skye 'gag yod dam/_gnyis pa sgyu ma la kun nas nyon mongs dang rnam byang yod dam/_gsum pa skye 'gag med kun nas nyon mongs dang rnam byang med pa de nges par 'byung 
[\[219a.2\]]bar 'gyur ram/_bzhi pa nye bar len pa'i phung po lnga la byang chub sems dpa' zhes brda dang tha snyad du btags pa yin nam/_lnga pa brda dang tha snyad tsam gyis nye bar len pa'i phung po 'di skye ba dang 'gag par 'gyur ram/_drug pa brda dang tha snyad med lus ngag yid gsum med skye 'gag med pa de gsum 
[\[219a.3\]]la bslabs na rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid du nges par 'byung bar 'gyur ram/_bdun pa phung po lnga po 'di dag yum la bslabs shing nges par 'byung bar 'gyur ram/_brgyad pa 'khrul pa'i dpe brgyad lta bu'i phung po lnga po 'di dag yum la bslabs nas nges par 'byung bar 'gyur ram/_dgu pa 
[\[219a.4\]]phung po lnga po ni 'khrul pa'i dpe brgyad lta bu ste yum la slob pa dang nges par 'byung ba'ang med do/_/rab 'byor gyis gsol pa/_de skad bstan cing bshad na 'jigs shing dngang bar mi 'gyur lags sam/_bka' stsal pa/_thabs mkhas pa'i dge ba'i bshes gnyen dang phrad na mi 'jigs/_thabs mi 
[\[219a.5\]]mkhas pa'i sdig pa'i grogs po dang phrad zin na 'jigs so/_/sems mi 'gong ba nyid sogs kyis/_/ngo bo nyid med sogs ston byed/_/de yi mi mthun phyogs bor ba/_/rnam pa kun tu yongs 'dzin yin/_/zhes pa la/_thabs mkhas pa ni byang chub sems dpa' yum la spyod pa'i tshe/_rnam pa thams 
[\[219a.6\]]cad mkhyen pa nyid dang ldan pa'i sems kyis phung po lnga mi rtag pa dang /_sdug bsngal ba dang /_bdag med pa dang /_zhi ba dang /_stong pa dang /_mtshan ma med pa dang /_smon pa med pa dang /_mngon par 'du bya ba med pa dang /_dben zhes bya bar so sor rtog ste de mi dmigs so/_/de bzhin du phung po lnga chos 
[\[219a.7\]]dgu'i rnam par so sor rtog ste de mi dmigs so/_/de nyid ldog pa'i sgo nas pha rol tu phyin pa drug gi thabs su ston pa las/_phung po lnga chos dgu po mi dmigs pa'i tshul gyis chos ston pa ni sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa'o/_/phung po lnga chos dgu po mi dmigs pa'i tshul gyis yid la byed cing nyan rang gi yid la byed 
[\[219b\]]
[\[219b.1\]]pa dang mi ldan pa ni tshul khrims kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'o/_/phung po lnga chos dgu po de nyid mi dmigs pa'i tshul gyis yid la mos shing bzod pa ni bzod pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa'o/_/phung po lnga chos dgu po de nyid kyi mi dmigs pa'i tshul gyis yid la byed cing rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi sems mi gtong 
[\[219b.2\]]bar spro ba ni brtson 'grus kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'o/_/phung po lnga chos dgu po de nyid mi dmigs pa'i tshul gyis yid la byed cing nyan thos dang rang sangs rgyas kyi yid la byed pa dang mi ldan pa dang /_de ma yin pa byang chub kyi gegs su gyur pa mi dge ba gzhan gyi go mi 'byed pa ni bsam gtan gyi pha rol tu 
[\[219b.3\]]phyin pa'o/_/stong pa nyid kyis gzugs stong pa ma yin gyi gzugs nyid kyang stong pa nyid/_stong pa nyid kyang gzugs so/_/de bzhin du sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa bco brgyad kyi bar ro/_/thabs mkhas pa de dang ldan pa'i byang chub sems dpa' ni bstan pa 'di thos na skrag par mi 'gyur ro/_/dge 
[\[219b.4\]]ba'i bshes gnyen ni gzugs nas rnam mkhyen gyi bar du chos dgu mi dmigs pa'i tshul gyis sems can rnams la chos ston cing /_dge ba'i rtsa ba thams cad kyang rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid ma gtogs par nyan thos dang rang sangs rgyas kyi sar yongs su bsngor mi 'jug pa'o/_/dge ba'i 
[\[219b.5\]]bshes gnyen gyis yongs su zin pa'i byang chub sems dpa' ni skrag par mi 'gyur ro/_/thabs mi mkhas pa ni byang chub sems dpa' yum la spyod pa'i tshe pha rol tu phyin pa drug bsgom zhing dmigs te dmigs pa'i tshul gyis rlom sems su byed pa dang /_rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid yid la byed pa 
[\[219b.6\]]dang mi ldan pas gzugs stong pa bco brgyad do zhes bya bar dmigs shing dmigs pa'i tshul gyis rlom sems su byed pa dang /_de bzhin du rnam mkhyen gyi bar la'o/_/des bstan pa 'di thos nas 'jigs shing dngang skrag go/_/sdig pa'i grogs po ni sangs rgyas kyi cha byad du byas pa drug_/nyan 
[\[219b.7\]]thos pha ma mkhan slob dge slong gi cha byad dang bcus ston pa las/_dang po dbyes 'dzud pa'i bdud ni pha rol tu phyin pa la mos pa med par byed de/_'di ni de bzhin gshegs pas gsungs pa ma yin/_chos dang 'dul ba ma yin/_rang bzor byas pa ste 'di dag la nyan pa la sogs par mi bya'o/_/gnyis pa 
[\[220a\]]
[\[220a.1\]]ni dgos pa med par bdud sdig can byang chub sems dpa'i drung du 'ongs nas pha rol tu phyin pas khyod la ci zhig bya zer ba'o/_/gsum pa kha 'dren pa'i bdud kyi las ni nyan rang gi gsung rab la snyan par brjod pa'o/_/bzhi pa byang chub sems dpa' sun 'byin pa'i bdud kyi las ni khyod la 
[\[220a.2\]]byang chub kyi sems cung zad kyang med na phyir mi ldog pa yang min pas sangs kyang mi rgya'o zhes zer ba'o/_/lnga pa stong pa hang po'i bdud kyi las ni mig ni bdag gam bdag gis stong /_sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa'i bar ni bdag gam bdag gis stong na sangs rgyas pas khyod la ci zhig bya zhes zer ba'o/_/drug pa chad lta'i 
[\[220a.3\]]bdud kyi las ni phyogs bcu dang sangs rgyas nyan thos byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyis stong ste/_sangs rgyas la sogs pa med ces zer ba'o/_/bdun pa ni bdud sdig can nyan thos kyi cha byad du byas nas rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid dang ldan pa'i yid la bya ba zlog cing /_nyan rang gi yid la bya ba 'debs 
[\[220a.4\]]shing ston pa'o/_/brgyad pa mkhan po dang slob dpon gyi cha byad du byas shing byang chub sems kyi spyod pa bzlog nas nyan thos kyi byang chub mngon du gyis shig_/thams cad mkhyen pas ci zhig bya zhes zer ba'o/_/dgu pa pha ma'i cha byad du byas nas 'khor ba na rkang lag dang mgo bo bcad pa med pas nyon mongs par 'gyur 
[\[220a.5\]]gyi zhes pa'o/_/bcu pa dge slong gi cha byad du byas nas gzugs nas rnam mkhyen gyi bar du chos dgu dmigs pa'i tshul gyis chos ston pa ni sdig pa'i grogs por rig par bya ste/_shes nas kyang yongs su spang bar bya'o/_/sgyu ma lta bu'i le'u ste bdun pa'o//_//le'u 'di nas rigs can byang chub sems dpa' 
[\[220a.6\]]bcu gsum/_dmigs pa bcu gcig_/ched du bya ba gsum/_go cha'i sgrub pa sum cu so drug_/'jug pa'i sgrub pa dgu/_tshogs kyi sgrub pa bcu tham pa ston no/_/de la rigs can byang chub sems dpa' ma grub par ston pa ni/_rtog pa yi ni chos drug dang /_/gnyen po dang ni spong ba dang /_/de dag yongs 
[\[220a.7\]]su gtugs pa dang /_/shes rab snying brtser bcas pa dang /_/slob ma thun mong min nyid dang /_/gzhan gyi don gyi [{]go rims [,]go rim [}]dang /_/ye shes rtsol ba mi mnga' bar/_/'jug pa'i rten la rigs shes bya/_/mdo spyir bstan pa ni rab 'byor gyis gsol pa/_byang chub sems dpa'i tshig gi don gang lags/_bka' 
[\[220b\]]
[\[220b.1\]]stsal pa/_tshig gi don med de/_byang chub dang sems can skye ba med pa'i phyir nam mkha' la bya'i rjes med pa de bzhin byang chub sems dpa' la yang tshig gi don med do/_/drod kyi byang chub sems dpa' ma grub pa'i dpe ni rmi lam la sogs pa 'khrul pa'i dpe brgyad la tshig gi gzhi'i don med dos bstan/_rtse mo'i 
[\[220b.2\]]byang chub sems dpa' ma grub pa'i dpe ni chos nyid brgyad la tshig gi don med dos bstan/_bzod pa'i byang chub sems dpa' ma grub pa'i dpe ni sgyu ma'i gzugs nas sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa'i bar la tshig gi gzhi'i don med dos bstan/_chos mchog gi byang chub sems dpa' ma grub pa'i dpe ni gzugs 
[\[220b.3\]]nas sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa bco brgyad kyi bar la tshig gi gzhi'i don med de/_de rnams med pa'i phyir ros bstan/_mthong lam gyi byang chub sems dpa' ma grub pa'i dpe ni 'dus byas dang 'dus ma byas la gzhi med dos bstan/_bsgom lam gyi byang chub sems dpa' ma grub pa'i dpe ni gzugs la 
[\[220b.4\]]skye ba med pa dang 'gag pa med pa dang kun nas nyon mongs pa dang rnam par byang ba dang mngon par 'du bya ba dang 'byung ba med pa la gnas med do/_/de bzhin du sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa'i bar du gnas med gzhi med dos bstan/_gnyen po'i byang chub sems dpa' ma grub pa'i dpe ni nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor 
[\[220b.5\]]'char ba la mun pa'i gnas med do/_/spong ba'i byang chub sems dpa' ma grub pa'i dpe ni bskal pa'i me 'bar ba'i tshe 'du byed kyi gnas med do/_/de dag yongs su gtugs pa'i byang chub sems dpa' ma grub pa'i dpe ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i sangs rgyas la tshul khrims la sogs pa 'chal pa lnga'i gnas 
[\[220b.6\]]med do/_/shes rab snying brtser bcas pa ma grub pa'i dpe ni dper na nyi ma dang zla ba'i 'od la gnas med do/_/slob ma thun mong ma yin pa'i dpe ni gza' dang skar ma'i 'od la gzhi med do/_/gzhan gyi don gyi [{]go rims kyi [,]go rim gyi [}]byang chub sems dpa' ma grub pa'i dpe ni lha rnams kyi 'od la gnas med do/_/ye shes rtsol 
[\[220b.7\]]ba mi mnga' bar 'jug pa'i rten ni dper na de bzhin gshegs pa sangs rgyas 'od la gnas med do/_/de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' la tshig gi gzhi'i don med do/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_byang chub gang yin pa dang byang chub kyi sems gang yin pa de thams cad ldan pa yang ma yin zhes pa la sogs pa'o/_/byang chub 
[\[221a\]]
[\[221a.1\]]sems dpa' chos thams cad la bslab par bya'o/_/de yang mgon pos/_chos kyi dbyings la dbyer med phyir/_/rigs ni tha dad rung ma yin/_/brten pa'i chos kyi bye brag gis/_/de yi dbye ba yongs su brjod/_/ces pa ni chos nyid la dbye ba med/_chos nyid rtogs pa'i gang zag 
[\[221a.2\]]la phye ba yin/_dper na bum pa la dbye ba med kyang bum par blugs pa'i chu dang 'o ma dang sbrang rtsi la sogs pas/_chu'i bum pa dang sbrang rtsi'i bum pa zhes pa lta bu'o/_/dmigs pa chos rnams thams cad de/_/de yang dge la sogs pa yin/_/'jig rten pa yi rtog pa dang /_/gang dag 'jig rten 'das 'dod dang /_/
[\[221a.3\]]zag bcas zag pa med chos dang /_/gang dag 'dus byas 'dus ma byas/_/slob ma thun mong chos rnams dang /_/gang dag thub pa'i thun mong min/_/zhes pa ni kun rdzob tu bstan gyi/_byang chub sems dpa' yum la spyod pas mi dmigs pa la bslab bo/_/ched du bya ba ni/_sems can kun mchog nyid 
[\[221a.4\]]sems dang /_/spong dang rtogs dang gsum po la/_/chen po gsum gyi rang byung gi_/ched du bya ba nyid ces brjod/_/chen po lnga ldan gyi bshad pa la sems dpa' che ba ni rab 'byor gyis gsol pa/_ci'i slad du byang chub sems dpa' la sems dpa' chen po zhes bgyi/_bka' stsal pa/_sems can gyi phung 
[\[221a.5\]]po chen po nges pa'i phul byed pas sems dpa' chen po zhes bya'o/_/phung po nges pa ni 'phags pa'i gang zag rnams so/_/rdo rje lta bu'i sems dang /_rlabs po che'i sems dang /_mi 'gul ba'i sems dang /_phan bde bskyed pa'i sems dang /_chos la dga' ba'i sems kyis sems can gyi don byed pa'i phyir 
[\[221a.6\]]na sems dpa' chen po zhes bgyi'o/_/spong ba che ba ni shA ri'i bus gsol pa/_bdag kyang byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po zhes bgyi ba de la spobs pa skye'o/_/bka' stsal pa/_spobs pa skyed cig_/sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po zhes bgyi ba ni byed pa'i skyes bu bcu gnyis dang /_yod med dang /_
[\[221a.7\]]rtag chad dang gzugs nas sangs rgyas dang byang chub kyi bar du lta ba spong ba'i phyir na sems dpa' chen po zhes bgyi'o/_/rab 'byor gyis smras pa/_ci'i phyir na gzugs nas sangs rgyas byang chub kyi bar du lta bar 'gyur ba yin/_shA ri'i bus smras pa/_gzugs nas mya ngan las 'das pa'i bar du dmigs shing 
[\[221b\]]
[\[221b.1\]]dmigs pa'i tshul gyis lta ba bskyed do/_/de'i phyir na gzugs nas mya ngan las 'das pa'i bar du lta bar 'gyur ba yin pas de spong ba'i phyir chos ston no/_/rtogs pa che ba ni rab 'byor gyis gsol pa/_sems dpa' chen po'i don de la bdag kyang spro ba skye'o/_/bka' stsal pa/_spobs par gyis shig_/

[\[251b.2\]]mong ma yin mtshan nyid dang /_/ma chags pa dang mi dmigs dang /_/mtshan ma smon lam zad pa dang /_/de yi rtags dang tshad med dang /_/thabs la mkhas pa rnam pa bcu/_/zhes pa la/_yul dang sbyor ba'i thabs la mkhas pa ni stong pa nyid la 'dris par bya'i mngon du ni mi bya'o/_/sangs rgyas

[\[251b.4\]]bar khyim du 'ong ba'i dpes sems can rnams 'khor ba las sgrol lo/_/gnas pa med pa'i thabs la mkhas pa'i dpes ni bya nam mkha' la 'phur nas der yang mi gnas sa la yang mi ltung ba'i dpes/_mtshan ma la yang mi spyod mtshan ma med pa mngon du yang mi byed do/_/dge ba'i rtsa ba shugs 
[\[251b.5\]]'phel ba'i dpe mda' brgyud mar long btags pas sa la ltung ba'i skabs mi 'byed pa'i dpes tshogs ma rdzogs par stong nyid dam pa thob par mi byed do/_/thun mong ma yin par thabs la mkhas pa ni snying rjes sems can gyi don byed pa la skyo dub med par sems can 'di dag ni byed pa'i skyes bu bcu gnyis

[\[252a.1\]]thabs la mkhas pa ni sems can 'di dag yun ring po nas smon lam la spyod pa ste/_brgya byin la mngon par dga' ba gzugs nas rnam mkhyen gyi bar la mngon par dga' ba'i smon lam gyi nyes pa spang ba'i phyir smon pa med pa'i chos ston pa'o/_/tshad med pa'i thabs la

[\[252b\]]
[\[252b.1\]]rab pha rol phyin pa la mtshan nyid med pa de bzhin du chos thams cad la spyi dang rang gi mtshan nyid med pa'o/_/rdzogs pa'i byang chub dang nye ba'i rtags ni bdag tu 'dzin pas 'khor zhing 'khyams pas bdag med pa ni rnam par byang ba de rdzogs pa'i byang chub dang nye ba'o/_/rnam par 'phel ba ni/_'dzam

[\[252b.7\]]rtog pa spang ba'i le'u ste zhe drug pa'o//_//dge ba bcu la sogs pa zil gyis gnon pa'i stong gsum gyi sems can rnams dge ba bcu tshad med pa bzhi/_gzugs med pa'i snyoms par 'jug pa bzhi la bkod pa bas/_rigs kyi bu yum 'dzin pa la sogs pa khyad par du 'phags pa'o/_/

[\[253a.1\]]lha'i rigs thams cad 'ong ba ni rigs kyi bu yum 'dzin pa la sogs pa'i drung du lha'i bu rnams nye bar 'ong ba'o/_/bdud kyi bar chad yong ba'i gang zag bcu gnyis ni/_ma mos pa dang /_the tshom za ba dang /_dge bshes dang bral ba dang /_yum ma yin pa la slob pa

[\[253b.1\]]'gags na dbang po gzhan 'gags pas ldog pa'i dpes yum du dge ba thams cad 'dus pa'o/_/phun sum tshogs pa thob pa ni yum yod na dge ba thams cad phun sum tshogs pa'o/_/rdzogs pa'i byang chub dang nye ba ni nyan thos dang rang sangs rgyas dang rang la rdzogs pa'i byang chub dang nye

[\[253b.7\]]dbye bas/_/so sor de dag dgu bdag nyid/_/gal te gzung don de bzhin med/_/de dag gang gis 'dzin par 'dod/_/ces pa la/_so so'i skye bo'i rnam rtog rdzas su 'dod pa dang /_'phags pa'i rnam rtog btags pa gnyis ni dbye ba'o/_/gzung ba med par 'dzin pa ni/_/phyin ci log ste rnam rtog go/_/
[\[254a\]]
[\[254a.1\]]de re re la'ang dgu dgu'o/_/'jug pa'i phyogs kyi rnam rtog rgyas bshad ni/_ngo bo nyid dang rigs dang ni/_/lam ni yang dag 'grub pa dang /_/shes pa'i dmigs pa ma 'khrul dang /_/mi mthun phyogs dang gnyen po dang /_/rang gis rtogs dang byed pa dang /_/de yi las byas 
[\[254a.2\]]'bras bu la/_/'jug pa'i phyogs kyi rten can gyi/_/rnam rtog der ni rnam dgur 'dod/_/ces pa ni ngo bo nyid kyi rnam rtog ni rab 'byor gyis smras pa/_sgyu ma lta bu'i sems ji ltar 'tshang rgya lags kyi lan khyod kyis sgyu ma dang sgyu ma lta bu'i sems dang /_de las gzhan pa yang dag par rjes su mthong ngam/_rab 
[\[254a.3\]]'byor gyis gsol pa ma mthong lags/_de cis na kun nas nyon mongs dang rnam byang gi chos thams cad med cing mi dmigs pas sangs rgya ba ma mchis so/_/de cis na gzugs nas rnam mkhyen gyi bar ni shin tu dben pa'o/_/rigs la rnam par rtog pa ni nam mkha' dang sprul pa dang gzugs brnyan

[\[254b\]]
[\[254b.1\]]pa ni dka' ste/_de bas kyang sems can thams cad bla na med pa'i byang chub la dgod par 'dod pa ni dka'o/_/de cis na nam mkha' med pa dang stong pa dang dben pa dang gsog dang gsob bzhin du sems can med pa'i don byed pa ni nam mkha' dang rtsod par 'dod pa nam mkha' 'dul bar 'dod pa'o/_/

[\[254b.3\]]byang chub pa'o/_/byed pa'i rnam rtog ni de bzhin nyid kyang med de de las gzhan pa med na/_de bzhin nyid la gnas nas sangs rgyas nas chos bstan nas lung ston pa gang yang med do/_/chags pa med pa'i le'u ste lnga bcu pa'o//_//las su bya ba'i rnam rtog ni rab 'byor ni ci dang ci ston pa de 
[\[254b.4\]]thams cad stong pa nyid las brtsams shing chos ston pas na/_sbyin pa nas rnam mkhyen gyi bar du yang med na sbyin pa la sogs pa la spyod pa lta ci smos/_'bras bu'i rnam par rtog pa ni 'phags pa rab 'byor gyis gnas pa des byang chub sems dpa' yum la spyod pa'i char yang mi phod do/_/de la dge 
[\[254b.5\]]slong brgya drug cu tsam mchod pa phul nas lung bstan pa'o/_/byang chub sems dpa'i mthong lam dngos la drug ste/_byang chub kyi rgyu dang /_gzhan zad pa shes pa dang /_mi skye ba shes pa dang /_byang chub yin pa dang /_rten 'brel rang bzhin med pa la zad pa dang mi skye ba med pa dang /_yod par 'dod pa

[\[255a\]]
[\[255a.1\]]yongs su gtad pa ni kun dga' bo/_shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa 'di khyod la gtad kyi chud ma gsan par zung shig_/'di bzung na dus gsum gyi byang chub gzung bar 'gyur ro/_/nga ston pa yin pa bzhin du 'di yang ston pa yin no/_/'di la mchod pa la sogs

[\[255a.7\]]pa ni byang chub sems dpa' phyir ldog pa thams cad ni rten cing 'brel par 'byung ba skye ba med pa dang zad pa med pa ma rtogs pas phyir ldog pa'o/_/'khrul pa med pa la 'khrul par ngo shes pa'i yum ni/_'di la bsal ba ci yang med/_/bsnan par bya ba cung zad med/_/yang dag nyid la yang dag lta/_/yang dag 
[\[255b\]]
[\[255b.1\]]mthong na rnam par grol/_/zhes pas/_byang chub sems dpa' yum la spyod pas/_byed pa'i skyes bu bcu gnyis gzugs nas rnam mkhyen gyi bar du rtag pa dang mi rtag pa/_bde ba dang sdug bsngal/_bdag dang bdag med/_zhi ba dang ma zhi bar yang dag par rjes su mi mthong ste/_de cis na shes rab kyi pha rol tu

[\[255b.3\]]bsdus pa gang /_/skad cig gcig gis bzod pa yis/_/bsdus pa de 'dir mthong ba'i lam/_/zhes pa ni pha rol tu phyin pa re re la drug drug go/_/skabs 'dir bsgom lam gyi ting nge 'dzin ni/_de nas seng ge rnam bsgyings pa'i/_/ting nge 'dzin la zhes pa la sogs so/_/thod rgal gyi le'u ste lnga bcu gsum 
[\[255b.4\]]pa'o//_//le'u 'di nas mthong lam gyi rtog pa dgu gsum bsgom lam gyi rtog pa dgu bzhi ston/_de la mthong lam gyi ldog pa'i phyogs kyi rtog pa ni/_srid dang zhi bar ltung phyir dang /_/rtogs pa dman pa nyid dang ni/_/yongs su 'dzin pa med pa dang /_/lam gyi rnam pa ma tshang dang /_/gzhan 
[\[255b.5\]]gyi rkyen gyis 'gro ba dang /_/ched du bya ba log pa dang /_/nyi tshe ba dang sna tshogs nyid/_/gnas dang 'jug la rmongs pa dang /_/rjes la 'gro zhes bya ba la/_/rnam rtog dgu yi bdag nyid 'di/_/ldog pa'i phyogs kyi rten can de/_/nyan thos la sogs yid las 'byung /_/zhes pa la/_/srid dang zhi 
[\[255b.6\]]bar mi ltung ba ni nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor ni gling bzhi la mthun par 'jug go'i dpe dang /_rtogs pa dman pa nyid ni rin po che sna bdun dang bral na 'khor los sgyur ba'i ming mi thob pa'i dpe dang /_/yongs su 'dzin pa med pa ni khyim thab med pa'i bud med 'phyon ma rnams kyis tshogs pa'i dpe dang /_lam gyi rnam pa 
[\[255b.7\]]ma tshang ba ni go mtshon tshang ba'i dpa' bo gzhan gyis mi thub pa'i dpe dang /_gzhan gyi rkyen gyis 'gro ba ni mkhar gyi rgyal po rnams 'khor los sgyur ba'i drung du nang nub 'gro ba'i dpe dang /_ched du bya ba log pa ni chu rnams gang gA dang 'grogs na rgya mtsho chen po rdol ba'i dpe dang /_nyi tshe ba ni thabs rkyang pa 
[\[256a\]]
[\[256a.1\]]lag pa g.yon pa dang 'dra ba'i dpe dang /_sna tshogs nyid ni chu klung rnams rgya mtshor phyin na ro gcig tu 'gyur ba'i dpe dang /_gnas dang 'jug la rmongs pa ni 'khor los sgyur ba'i rgyal po'i rjes su rin po che sna bdun 'brang ba'i dpe yum gyi rjes su pha rol tu phyin pa lnga 'brang ba ni byang chub

[\[260a.4\]]mthong ltar/_/chos rnams gnyis su med par ni/_/skad cig ma ni gcig gis mthong /_/zhes pa ni rmi lam dang sgyu ma la sogs pa lta bu'i nyer len gyi phung po lnga la gnas nas pha rol tu phyin pa drug rdzogs pa ni mi mthong rmi lam rmi ba po mi mthong zhes pa la sogs pas bstan/_

[\[260a.6\]]mtshan nyid med pa'i le'u ste drug cu gnyis pa'o//_//le'u 'di nas 'gro rnams zhi ba dang /_bsdu ba'i dngos po bzhi ston pa ni zhus lan drug_/phyogs bskor bdun/_bsdu ba'i dngos po bzhi ste bzhi bcu ston pa las/_'gro rnams zhi ba'i las dang ni/_/bsdu rnams 
[\[260a.7\]]bzhi la 'god pa dang /_/kun nas nyon mongs bcas pa yis/_/rnam par byang ba rtogs pa dang /_/bdag nyid ji bzhin sems can gyi/_/don dang pha rol phyin drug dang /_/sangs rgyas lam dang rang bzhin gyis/_/stong pa nyid dang gnyis zad dang /_/brda dang dmigs pa med 
[\[260b\]]
[\[260b.1\]]pa dang /_/lus can rnams ni yongs smin dang /_/byang chub sems dpa'i lam dang ni/_/mngon par zhen pa bkag pa dang /_/byang chub thob dang sangs rgyas zhing /_/rnam par dag dang nges pa dang /_/sems can don ni tshad med dang /_/sangs rgyas bsten sogs yon tan dang /_/byang chub 
[\[260b.2\]]yan lag las rnams ni/_/chud mi za dang bden mthong dang /_/phyin ci log ni spongs pa dang /_/de bzhin med pa'i tshul dang ni/_/rnam par byang dang tshogs dang ni/_/'dus byas dang ni 'dus ma byas/_/tha dad nyid du mi mthong dang /_/mya ngan 'das la 'god pa ni/_/chos kyi sku yi 
[\[260b.3\]]phrin las te/_/rnam pa nyi shu bdun du 'dod/_/ces pa las/_dang po ni chos thams cad sgyu ma lta bu dngos po ma mchis pa rang gi mtshan nyid kyis stong pa la sems bskyed nas sems can gyi don mi 'thad do zhus pa'i lan/_chos thams cad mtha' las 'das pa stong pa nyid dang /_
[\[260b.4\]]thog ma dang tha ma med pa stong pa nyid yin yang /_byis pa so so'i skye bo rnams 'khrul nas/_rmi lam mthong ngo rmi lam rmi ba po yang mthong ngo la sogs te/_dge mi dge'i las kyis 'gro drug gi snang ba shar bas byang chub sems dpa' don mdzad do/_/gnyis pa rab 'byor gyis smras 
[\[260b.5\]]pa/_chos thams cad stong pa nyid la sems can gyi don mdzad pa ni ngo mtshar lags so/_/bka' stsal pa/_chos thams cad rang bzhin med pa la sems bskyed nas pha rol tu phyin pa drug gis sems can gyi don mdzad pa ni ngo mtshar rmad kyi chos so/_/gsum pa rab 'byor gyis 
[\[260b.6\]]gsol pa/_nyan rang la ma mchis pa'i ngo mtshar rmad du byung ba'i chos de gang lags/_bka' stsal pa/_sbyin pa nas rnam mkhyen gyi bar ro/_/phyogs bskor bdun ni/_ngas sangs rgyas kyi spyan gyis phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten gyi khams la gzigs na/_byang chub sems dpa' 
[\[260b.7\]]rnams ched du bsams nas phyogs bcu'i dmyal ba/_phyogs bcu'i dud 'gro/_phyogs bcu'i [{]yi dgas[,]yi dwags[}]/_phyogs bcu'i 'dod lha/_phyogs bcu'i gzugs khams kyi lha/_phyogs bcu'i mi rnams kyi don byed pa mthong ngo /_/bsdu ba bzhi ni zang zing gi sbyin pa dang /_chos 
[\[261a\]]
[\[261a.1\]]kyi sbyin pa dran pa nye bar gzhag pa nas sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa'i bar gyi chos sku dang /_mtshan bzang po sum cu rtsa gnyis dang /_dpe byad bzang po brgyad cu'i longs sku dang /_yi ges yi ge med pa'i don rtogs par gyis shig ces pa yan chod chos kyi sbyin 
[\[261a.2\]]pa dang pha rol tu phyin pa drug brjod pa ni snyan par smra ba dang /_de drug gi don la don spyod pa ni don spyod pa dang /_de drug dang mi 'gal ba ni don mthun pa'o/_/sprul pa'i sku'i zhus lan gsum ni rab 'byor gyis gsol pa/_chos thams cad stong pa nyid lags na/_byang chub sems

[\[261a.6\]]ba med do/_/gnod pa med do/_/zhus lan gnyis pa gzugs nas rnam mkhyen gyi bar yang gzhan de bzhin nyid kyang gzhan/_yang dag pa'i mtha' yang gzhan ma yin na/_las rgyu 'bras mi 'thad de/_chos kyi dbyings la rnam par dag pa ma mchis pa'i slad du'o zhes 
[\[261a.7\]]pa la/_bka' stsal pa/_las rgyu 'bras kun rdzob tu gsungs kyi/_don dam par ma yin no/_/zhus lan gsum pa bsgoms pa dang ma bsgoms pas rgyun du zhugs pa la sogs pa'i 'phags pa'i gang zag char phye nas bstan pa ma yin na/_kun sbyor gsum spangs pa'i phyir rgyun zhugs 
[\[261b\]]
[\[261b.1\]]thob po zhes bya ba la sogs pa ji ltar rtogs par bgyi zhus pa'i lan/_rgyun du zhugs pa la sogs pa 'dus byas dang 'dus ma byas mtshan nyid med par khong du chud pa'i tshe/_bdag nyid dge ba'i lam la spyod cing gzhan ma la 'god pa'i dpe ni sprul pa de dge ba spyod kyang de'i ro myang ba ni

[\[261b.4\]]gnyis pa gsol pa/_phung po lnga sprul pa lta bu lags na/_sprul pa la ni phung po lnga kun nas nyon mongs rnam par byang ba 'gro ba lnga ma mchis na ji ltar byang chub sems dpa'i mthur 'gyur lags/_lan byang chub sems dpa' byang chub kyi spyod pa'i tshe/_'gro ba lnga la sogs pa la dmigs 
[\[261b.5\]]te/_sprul pa lta bur shes so/_/mthong ngo /_/rtogs so/_/gsol pa sprul pa lta bur rtogs lags na gang gi don du byang chub sems dpa' sbyin pa nas rnam mkhyen gyi bar la spyod cing sangs rgyas kyi zhing dag sems can yongs su smin par bgyid pa lags/_lan gal te sems can rnams 
[\[261b.6\]]rang rang gis sprul pa lta bur ma shes pa'i don du sems can rnams kyi don mdzad do/_/gsum pa chos thams cad rmi lam lta bu la sogs pa lags na sems can rnams ci la gnas na byang chub sems dpa' rnams yum la spyod cing sems can rnams gyen du bskyod pa lags/_lan sems 
[\[261b.7\]]can rnams ni ming dang mtshan ma med pa la zhen pa lags/_de'i log rtogs 'gegs pa'o/_/bdag nyid ji bzhin sems can gyi don byed pa la gnyis las dang po gsol pa/_chos thams cad ming dang mtshan mar bas la/_chos su rtogs pa las byung ba lags na byang chub sems dpa' 
[\[262a\]]
[\[262a.1\]]yum la spyod pa'i tshe/_bdag gzhan dge la spyod cing sems can rnams theg pa gsum la gzhol ba lags/_lan thal ba ni gal te dngos po'am dngos po'i mtshan ma gcig yod na ni/_byang chub sems dpa' yum la spyod pa'i tshe/_bdag gzhan dge ba

[\[262a.3\]]'dres pa'i bar rang rdzogs par byas nas gzhan dge ba la sbyor te/_gal te chung ngu na skra'i rtse mo cha shas bzhag pa tsam cig yod kyang byang chub sems dpa' yum la spyod pa'i tshe/_mtshan ma med pa dran pa med pa yid la byed pa med par sangs rgya bar mi 'gyur te/_sems can rnams

[\[262b.1\]]na/_byang chub sems dpa' yum la spyod pas/_ji ltar gzugs nas rnam mkhyen gyi bar la med pa la bslab par bgyi/_tha dad med pa la tha dad du bslabs na phyin ci log tu mi 'gyur ram/_de ci'i phyir zhe na/_gzugs nas rnam mkhyen gyi bar yang gzhan ma yin/_chos kyi dbyings

[\[263a.1\]]do/_/rang bzhin gyis stong pa nyid kyi mdzad pa la zhus lan gnyis las/_dang po ni gal te chos thams cad rang bzhin gyis stong pa lags na rang bzhin gyis stong pa nyid la ni sems can dang chos dang chos ma yin pa yang ma dmigs na/_byang chub sems dpa' rnams ji ltar thams cad mkhyen

[\[263b.6\]]pa'am 'gro sa med de/_gzhan du na 'jig rten rnams ni rmongs shing phung po la mngon par zhen pas skye rga na 'chi'i sdug bsngal las mi grol lo/_/de'i phyir byang chub sems dpas rang bzhin stong pa nyid kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la spyod cing /_gzugs nas rnam mkhyen gyi bar stong

[\[264a.6\]]so/_/lan phar phyin nas rnam mkhyen gyi bar ma rdzogs na sangs mi rgya rdzogs na sangs rgya zhes pa de bas gzugs nas rnam mkhyen gyi bar de yang rang bzhin la gnas te sangs rgya'o/_/rang bzhin de yang nye bar zhi ba ste/_'phel 'grib med/_skye 'gag med/_kun nas nyon

[\[264b.6\]]lan gzugs nas rnam mkhyen gyi bar la mi sbyor mi 'byed de/_gang sbyar bar bya ba'am dbye bar bya ba ngo bo nyid med pa'i phyir ro/_/zhus lan gsum pa ni gang sbyar ba'am dbye bar bya ba'i ngo bo nyid ma mchis na/_yum la ji ltar bslab par bgyi/_yum la ma bslabs na sangs 
[\[264b.7\]]rgya bar mi nus lags so/_/de'i lan de de bzhin te thabs la mkhas pa yin gyi 'on te ma dmigs na/_'di ni gzugs nas rnam mkhyen gyi bar ro zhes ci zhig gzung /_sbyin pa nas rnam mkhyen gyi bar ni gzung du med pa ste/_slob pa dang byang chub dang sangs rgyas nas so so'i skye

[\[265a.1\]]bo'i bar ro zhes yod par ga la 'gyur/_zhus pa bzhi pa ni chos thams cad dngos po ma mchis pa la so so'i skye bo nas sangs rgyas kyi bar du dmigs su ma mchis lags/_lan ji ltar gzugs nas rnam mkhyen gyi bar la byis pa so so'i skye bos mngon 
[\[265a.2\]]par zhen par 'gyur ba de ltar yod par 'gyur tam/_yod dam yod par 'gyur ram/_gsol pa de rnams phyin ci log lags kyi de ni ma mchis lags so/_/lan chos thams cad dngos po med pa la phyin ci log gi dbang gis dngos por bzung ba de byang chub sems dpa' thabs la 
[\[265a.3\]]mkhas pas de las bzlog nas byang chub la 'jog go/_/zhu ba lnga pa ni byang chub la 'jug pa'i thabs la mkhas pa de gang lags/_lan byang chub sems dpa' yum la spyod pa'i tshe chos gang la thogs pa'i dngos po'am zhum pa'am/_sgyid lug pa'am/_tshi chad nas le lor gyur pa'i 
[\[265a.4\]]dngos po ma mthong ste/_sems can rnams rmongs pas phung po khams skye mched rten 'brel la mngon par zhen pas/_byang chub sems dpa' chos thams cad mtshan nyid med par yang dag par rjes su mthong nas/_sgyu ma'i mkhan po lta bu'i lus kyis phar phyin drug gi mi mthun 
[\[265a.5\]]pa'i phyogs las bzlog ste/_rgyun du zhugs pa la sogs pa la bkod do/_/zhus pa drug pa ni sems can gtan ma mchis na ji ltar pha rol tu phyin pa drug la sbyor zhing /_rgyun zhugs nas rnam mkhyen gyi bar du thob par 'gyur lags/_lan kun rdzob kyi brdas de tshe ston pa ma gtogs 
[\[265a.6\]]par byang chub sems dpa' tshe 'di nyid la gdags kyang mi dmigs na rgyun zhugs pa la sogs pa lta ci smos/_zhus pa bdun pa ni chos gcig gam tha dad kyang mi dmigs/_khams gsum dang 'dus byas dang 'dus ma byas dang /_sems can du gdags pa mi dmigs na sems can 
[\[265a.7\]]bcings grol kun nas nyon mongs dang rnam byang las kyi rgyud tha dad pa las dang nyon mongs rnam par smin pas bdag gam sems can thams cad rgyun chad par mi 'gyur lags sam/_de'i lan sngon sems can 'gro ba lnga sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems dpa' yod pa phyis med par 'gyur ba 
[\[265b\]]
[\[265b.1\]]med de/_de bzhin gshegs pa ni byung rung ma byung rung de bzhin nyid la sogs par gnas te/_de la byed pa'i skyes bu bcu gnyis phung po khams la sogs pa lta ci smos/_'gro ba lnga'i 'khor ba nas dgrol ba lta ga la yod/_byang chub sems dpas sngon gyi sangs rgyas la yang 
[\[265b.2\]]ngo bo nyid kyis stong pa de thos nas/_sems can phyin ci log gi skye bo rnams drang ba'i phyir byang chub la 'jug go/_/dper na sgyu ma'i mkhan po rnams sprul pa zhig gis srog chags mang po sprul te/_bza' ba dang bca' bas tshim par byas nas/_bdag gis bsod nams mang du 
[\[265b.3\]]spel lo zhes yang yang du brjod na/_des tshim par byas pa'am bsod nams mang du 'phel ba yod dam/_ma mchis lags so/_/lan byang chub sems dpa' sems dang po bskyed pa nas bzung ste/_sbyin pa nas ma 'dres pa bco brgyad la spyad kyang lam yongs su rdzogs pa 
[\[265b.4\]]dang sangs rgyas kyi zhing yongs su dag pa dang /_sems can yongs su smin pa dang /_sems can de lta bu btul ba gang yang med do/_/zhus lan gnyis pa lus can rnams yongs su smin par byed pa'i phrin las ni rab 'byor gyis gsol pa/_sems can yongs su smin pa 
[\[265b.5\]]dang sangs rgyas kyi zhing yongs su dag par bgyid pa'i byang chub [(]gyi [,]kyi [)]lam gang lags/_bka' stsal pa/_sbyin pa nas ma 'dres pa'i bar ni byang chub sems dpa'i lam yin te/_'di ltar sbyin pa po la sogs pa'i gang zag drug gis sems can pha rol tu phyin pa drug dang bral 
[\[265b.6\]]ba dag mthong na/_khyod ci'i phyir pha rol tu phyin pa drug la sogs pa la mi spyod/_de dag rnams kyis smras pa/_bdag cag rkyen dang mi ldan pa'i phyir ro/_/gang zag drug pos smras pa/_phar phyin drug gi rkyen/_zas 'dod pa la zas zhes pa nas mi'i yo byad gang yang rung ba'i bar 
[\[265b.7\]]du sbyin gyi/_phar phyin drug la sogs pa la spyod cig ces pa nas yon dag spel gtongs kyi phar phyin drug ste/_le'u re drug pa'o//_//le'u 'di nas mdzad pa bzhi ni byang chub sems dpa'i lam dang mngon par ma zhen pa dang /_byang chub thob pa dang /_sangs rgyas kyi zhing yongs su dag 
[\[266a\]]
[\[266a.1\]]pa'o/_/byang chub sems dpa'i lam ni sbyin pa nas rnam mkhyen gyi bar ro/_/gsol pa/_gal te chos thams cad stong pa lags na/_byang chub sems dpa' dmigs pa bcu gcig rgyun zhugs la sogs pa 'di dag ces bslabs na/_spros par mi 'gyur lags sam/_
[\[266a.2\](]len [,]lan [)]byis pa so so'i skye bos chos 'di dag stong par mi shes pas mngon par ma zhen pa'i mdzad pa ni byang chub sems dpa'i yum la spyod pa'i tshe chos thams cad mngon par 'du bya ba ma gtogs par gzugs nas rnam mkhyen gyi bar mi dmigs te/_ngo bo nyid stong pa'i phyir sems can rnams la sbyin 
[\[266a.3\]]pa nas rnam mkhyen la spyod cing des rlom sems su ma byed cig_/de la snying po gang yang med do zhes nas phar phyin drug la mi gnas/_bsam gtan bzhi nas mngon shes drug gi bar dang /_de mnyam par gzhag pa dang /_rnam pa gang gis mnyam par gzhag pa la mi gnas/_nyan rang gi byang 
[\[266a.4\]]chub thob kyang mi gnas te/_de ci'i phyir zhe na/_sems dang po bskyed pa nas bzung nas sa bcu'i bar du rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid ma yin pa sems gzhan gang yang ma bskyed do/_/byang chub thob pa'i mdzad pa ni/_gsol pa/_chos thams cad ma skyes lags na/_byang chub ji ltar bsgrub 
[\[266a.5\]]par bgyi/_de'i lan chos thams cad ma skyes pa yin yang de lta bu mi shes pa'i don du/_byang chub kyi lam gyi sems can rnams 'khor ba las thar bar byed do/_/skyes pa dang ma skyes pa dang /_gnyis ka dang gnyis ka ma yin pa la gnas nas byang chub thob par byed pa med pa ma yin 
[\[266a.6\]]gyi/_'jig rten gyi tha snyad kyis byang chub thob par bstan pa'o/_/sangs rgyas kyi zhing yongs su dag par byed pa'i mdzad pa ni gsol pa/_chos thams cad rang bzhin gyis dben pa lags na byang chub sems dpas sangs rgyas kyi zhing yongs su dag par bgyi/_de'i lan byang chub sems 
[\[266a.7\]]dpa' sems dang po bskyed pa nas lus ngag gi gnas ngan len sbyangs nas sangs rgyas kyi zhing dag par byed do/_/de la gnas ngan len ni mi dge ba bcu dang phar phyin drug gi mi mthun phyogs dang byang chub kyi phyogs la sogs pa dang bral ba dang /_nyan rang gi byang chub la 'dod pa bskyed cing mngon du 
[\[266b\]]
[\[266b.1\]]byed pa dang /_gzugs nas rga shi'i bar gyi 'du shes dang /_khams gsum dang /_dmigs pa bcu gcig gi 'du shes ni gnas ngan len no/_/gnas ngan len sbyong ba gsum ni bdag phar phyin drug la spyod cing gzhan yang skul ba dang /_zhing sbyong gnyis pa ni mchod pa rnam pa lngas sbyong 
[\[266b.2\]]ba dang /_gsum pa ni snyoms 'jug gsum/_byang phyogs sum cu rtsa bdun rnam thar sgo gsum la sems can thams cad dgod pa/_nam chos de dag thams cad dang ldan par ma gyur gyi bar du sangs mi rgya bar bdag dang gzhan rnams kyi tshogs sogs shing sangs rgyas kyi zhing dag par 
[\[266b.3\]]byed de de ci nas kyang ngan song gsum dug gsum mi rtag pa la sogs pa bzhi/_bag la nyal dang kun nas ldang bas phyin ci log tu gdags pa'i ming yang mi srid cing /_shing rlung gis btab pa'am phyi nang gi dngos po dag la rnam thar gsum dang /_skye 'gag med pa dang /_ngo bo nyid

[\[266b.6\]]mchog spangs pas/_shi ba'i 'og tu ngan song du 'gro ba de dag thugs rje chen pos bkrol nas byang chub la nges par sbyor ro/_/sangs rgyas kyi zhing dag pa'i le'u ste re bdun pa'o//_//le'u 'di nas mdzad pa bzhi ste/_nges pa dang sems can don dang /_sangs rgyas bsten la sogs 
[\[266b.7\]]pa dang /_byang chub kyi yan lag go/_/de la dang po nges pa ni byang chub sems dpa' sems bskyed pa la sogs pa bla na med pa'i byang chub tu nges pa'o/_/gsol pa/_byang chub sems dpa' nges pa ngan song gsum la sogs par 'gyur ram/_de'i lan mgo bsgre ni nyan rang gi byang chub ngan song 
[\[267a\]]
[\[267a.1\]]du mi 'gyur ba de bzhin du/_byang chub sems dpa' phar phyin drug la spyod pa ni mi khom pa'i gnas brgyad la sogs par ltung ba'i gnas med do/_/sems can don tshad med kyi mdzad pa la ni drug las/_dang po gsol pa/_sems dang po bskyed pa mi khom pa'i gnas brgyad du mi

[\[267a.5\]]rnam par rtog pa med la/_sgyu ma'i rta glang bzhin du gsod pa la sogs pa byung yang sdug bsngal med do/_/zhus lan bzhi pa de lta bu'i lus len kyang sdug bsngal gyis mi gos pa'i thabs la mkhas pa de gang lags/_de'i lan yum shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la spyod cing gos pa med de/_

[\[267a.7\]]med do/_/zhus lan drug pa shes phyin ngo bo nyid kyis stong na/_ji ltar khongs su 'du lags/_de la ni khongs su chud pa dang ma chud pa ma mchis so/_/de'i lan chos thams cad ni stong pa nyid yin pa la yin par rtogs pas khongs su chud pa'o/_/sangs rgyas bsten sogs kyi phrin las ni chos 
[\[267b\]]
[\[267b.1\]]thams cad stong pa la mngon shes drug gis phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten gyi khams kyi sangs rgyas la bsnyen bkur zhing /_sems can yongs su smin par bgyid pa lags/_de'i lan phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten gyi khams kyi sangs rgyas la sogs pa btags pa thams cad kyang rang bzhin gyis stong pas

[\[268b.1\]]gyi/_bden pa bzhi mnyam pa nyid gang yin pa ni mya ngan las 'das pa'o/_/zhus lan gsum pa mnyam pa nyid de gang lags kyi lan/_chos nyid bdun rang gi mtshan nyid kyis stong pa'i phyir ro/_/dngos po dang dngos po med pa'i le'u ste re dgu pa'o//_//le'u 'di nas phyin ci log

[\[269a\]]
[\[269a.1\]]bar dngos po dang rdzas su ma mchis lags na/_sbyin pa nas rnam mkhyen gyi bar la gnas nas byang chub tu sems bskyed/_de'i lan khyod kyis brjod pa'i chos de rnams 'khrul pa'i dpe drug lta bu ma yin snyam mam/_yang gsol pa chos de rnams 'khrul

[\[269a.3\]]snyam mam/_sbyin pa nas rnam mkhyen gyi bar gyi 'dus byas pas ni sangs rgya bar mi nus te/_'on kyang lam skyed par byed kyi 'bras bu thob pa'i phyir ni ma yin no/_/de rnams kyi skye ba med pa dang /_'byung ba med pa dang /_mtshan nyid med pa ni mya ngan las 'das pa'o/_/
[\[269a.4\]]'on kyang sems dang po bskyed pa nas nye bar bzung nas dge ba'i chos sbyin pa nas ma 'dres pa bco brgyad kyi bar du sgyu ma lta bu shes nas lam rdzogs par 'gyur ba'i phyir/_phar phyin nas rnam mkhyen gyi bar dang /_de ltar spyad pa'i byang chub sems dpa' rnams 'khrul pa'i dpe

[\[269a.7\]]pa med cing spros pa thams cad dang bral ba mnyam pa nyid ni sangs rgyas so/_/'dus byas dang 'dus ma byas dbyer med pa'i phrin las kyi mdzad pa lnga las/_dang po de bzhin gshegs pa chos thams cad kyi yum la dbang bsgyur ba ma lags sam/_lan ni chos thams cad kyang gzhan 
[\[269b\]]
[\[269b.1\]]la de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyang gzhan ma yin te/_so so'i skye bo nas sangs rgyas kyi de bzhin nyid la khyad med pa'i phyir ro/_/zhus lan gnyis pa/_chos thams cad kyi mnyam pa nyid la khyad ma mchis na/_ji ltar dkon mchog gsum 'jig rten du 'byung bar 'gyur lags/_

[\[269b.3\]]gshegs pa chos mnyam pa nyid las mi g.yo ba de bzhin du so so'i skye bo la sogs pa thams cad kyang chos mnyam pa nyid las mi g.yo ba lags sam/_lan de bzhin gshegs pa'i chos nyid brgyad gang yin pa de nyid so so'i skye bo la sogs pa thams cad kyi chos nyid do/_/
[\[269b.4\]]zhus lan bzhi pa so so'i skye bo nas de bzhin gshegs pa'i bar gyi chos nyid mnyam pa nyid lags na/_'di ltar mtshan nyid mi 'dra ba gzugs nas rnam mkhyen gyi bar gcig la gcig 'dus pa/_gzugs nas rnam mkhyen gyi bar gyi chos nyid gzhan dang gzhan la 
[\[269b.5\]]bslabs nas phar phyin drug rdzogs nas sems can smin par bgyid pa lags zer ba'i lan ni gzugs nas rnam mkhyen gyi bar gyi chos nyid stong pa nyid ma yin snyam mam/_gsol pa de lta ma lags so/_/stong pa nyid la gzugs nas rnam mkhyen gyi bar gyi mtshan nyid de yod 
[\[269b.6\]]snyam mam/_ma lags so/_/de lta bas na chos rnams kyi chos nyid gang yin pa de la so so'i skye bo nas 'phags pa'i gang zag rnams med/_so so'i skye bo nas 'phags pa'i gang zag las gud na'ang de med par rig par bya'o/_/zhus lan lnga pa chos nyid de 'dus byas lags 
[\[269b.7\]]sam/_'dus ma byas lags/_lan ni gnyis ka ma yin te/_'dus byas las gud na yang 'dus ma byas med/_don dam pa la lus ngag dang yid med/_de gsum las gud na'ang don dam pa med de/_chos thams cad kyi mnyam pa nyid gang yin pa ni don dam pa ste/_byang 
[\[270a\]]
[\[270a.1\]]chub sems dpa' yum la spyod pa'i tshe/_don dam pa las kyang mi g.yo la byang chub sems dpa'i spyad pa spyod do/_/don dam pa'i rnal 'byor gyi le'u ste don gcig pa'o//_//mya ngan las 'das pa'i mdzad pa las zhus lan drug las/_dang po gsol pa/_chos 
[\[270a.2\]]thams cad kyi mnyam pa nyid gang yin pa stong pa ste/_byed pa dang bya ba ma mchis kyang byang chub sems dpa' yum la spyod pa don dam pa las mi g.yo lags/_bsdu ba'i dngos po bzhis sems can gyi don bgyid lags/_lan ni don dam la ci yang med kyang sems can rang bzhin gyis stong pa 
[\[270a.3\]]nyid ma shes pa byed pa'i skyes bu bcu gnyis su 'du shes pa dang /_phung po dang khams dang skye mched dang rten 'brel dang 'dus byas dang 'dus ma byas kyi 'du shes dang phral nas mya ngan las 'das pa la 'god do/_/zhus lan gnyis pa/_gsol pa cis stong pa lags/_lan 'du shes thams cad kyis stong ste/_sprul 
[\[270a.4\]]pa cig sprul na de stong pa nyid ma yin pa'i dngos po gang yang med pas 'di ni sprul pa'o/_/'di ni stong pa'o zhes dmigs su med do/_/zhus lan gsum pa phung po lnga yang sprul pa'o/_/zhus lan bzhi pa 'jig rten las 'das pa'i chos dran pa nye bar gzhag pa bzhi nas sangs rgyas kyi bar yang sprul pa lags 
[\[270a.5\]]sam/_lan ni nyan thos rang rgyal byang chub sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs pa las dang nyon mongs pa'ang sprul pa'o/_/rgyun du zhugs pa spang ba thams cad kyang sprul pa'o/_/zhus lan lnga pa sprul pa dang ma sprul pa de gang lags/_de'i lan skye ba dang 'gag pa'i chos thams cad sprul pa/_ma skyes ma 'gags 
[\[270a.6\]]pa mi mnyam pa'i mya ngan las 'das pa ni ma sprul pa'o/_/zhus lan drug pa nyan thos la sogs pa gang gis kyang mdzad pa'i ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid de gang lags/_de'i lan sngon dngos po yod par gyur la/_phyis dngos po med par 'gyur ba'am/_rang gi dngos po'am/_gzhan gyi dngos po yang med na/_ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid
